Promise You Won't Leave Me

by CarmineJade

Summary

I've had a whole life of looking after my best friend. I honestly didn't mind it, but if this is her one chance at total happiness then I'm behind her 500%. Maybe when she's collared I can visit her and make sure that her affini is treating her right. I think she'd love that...

A trip to the voluntary domestication center is meant to help one very eager seed find her potential owner and become the happiest she's ever been. So then why is her best friend positively terrified the second she sets foot inside it?

See the end of the work for notes

Chapter 1: Day Tripping

“Good morning, Judy! Your little friend will be here in approximately [5 minutes] for her errands today!”

 

“Thanks, Hal. Really appreciate it.” The HabAI chirped positively at the gratitude while I fixed my appearance. Shaver in hand, I was delicately trimming my side shave while humming some song I probably heard years ago. The name would no doubt forever escape me, but it was probably one of the more forgivable corporate jingles from before the Compact took charge. It was just too catchy to stop humming.

Don’t keep her waiting~”

She earned a smile for that. I made sure to squeeze her hand right back before pressing a kiss to her temple. “Of course, Kells. Anything for you.” And I meant it. I adjusted my entire life for this girl. What's a few more big changes when my best friend’s happiness was on the line?

Chapter 2: A Search for Answers

Chapter Summary

Last chapter, Kelly filled out her domestication paperwork and received a very exciting email almost immediately the next day! But... so did Judy?

This chapter, the two friends set out to investigate just what exactly is going on here.

Chapter Notes

CW: The first cases in this story of xenodrugs being used quite liberally, teasing touches and caresses, and gesture training a very reluctant sophont. Idk how I went the whole first chapter without any xenodrugs.
